    '["How do you pick canned soup?","Low-sodium canned soup with a short list, can undented.","Sodium words on the front have fixed meanings. Deep dents, leaks, rust and bulging are what to skip on the can.","Read the sodium words on the front. Low sodium is a fixed ceiling, 140 mg or less. Reduced sodium means 25 percent less than a named soup. Pick a can without deep dents, leaks, rust or bulging.","Under 21 CFR 101.61, sodium words on a label have set meanings per standard serving. Sodium free means less than 5 mg. Very low sodium means 35 mg or less. Low sodium means 140 mg or less. Reduced sodium, or less sodium, means at least 25 percent less than a reference food, and the label names that food and the percent difference. Salt is not the same word as sodium; salt means sodium chloride. No salt added means no salt went in during processing, and a soup that is not sodium free says “not a sodium free food”. On the shelf, a slightly dented can is fine, but skip deep dents the size of a finger and dents with sharp points on the seam. Dusty cans or torn labels may mean old stock. Dates on cans mark peak quality, not safety. In a cool, dry pantry, high-acid canned food keeps 12 to 18 months for best quality, and low-acid canned food, including vegetable soups other than tomato, keeps 2 to 5 years.","A short ingredient list is a craft standard, not a safety line. The sodium words on the front each carry a fixed meaning.","Low sodium canned soup, undented can",["Low sodium is a fixed ceiling; reduced sodium only means less than a named soup.","Read the front for low sodium versus reduced sodium.","Check reduced sodium claims for the named soup they compare against.","Check each can for deep dents, seam dents, leaks, rust and bulging lids.","Store cans cool and dry, between 50 °F and 70 °F.","Put new cans behind older ones and use the oldest first.","Wipe the lid before opening.","Move opened soup to a covered glass or plastic container in the refrigerator.","Many food cans are lined with an epoxy made with BPA. Fresh or frozen soup has no can lining.","In a can, pick one that says BPA-free on the label over one that says nothing."],["“No salt added” is not “sodium free”; the soup can still carry sodium.","Leave cans with deep dents or dents on the seam on the shelf.","BPA-free is the maker’s word.
